ai.perplexity.comet
    set searchTerm to "
    set scanned to 0
    set found to false
    repeat with w in windows
        set tabIndex to 0
        repeat with t in tabs of w
            set tabIndex to tabIndex + 1
            set scanned to scanned + 1
            if (title of t as text) contains searchTerm then
                set active tab index of w to tabIndex
                set index of w to 1
                set found to true
                exit repeat
            end if
        end repeat
        if found then exit repeat
    end repeat
    activate
    if found then
        return "matched scanned=" & scanned
    else
        return "no-match scanned=" & scanned
    end if
end tell

                focus t
                set found to true
                exit repeat
            end if
        end repeat
        if found then exit repeat
    end repeat
    activate
    if found then
        return "matched scanned=" & scanned
    else
        return "no-match scanned=" & scanned
    end if
end tell

    set found to false
    repeat with w in windows
        repeat with s in spaces of w
            repeat with t in tabs of s
                set scanned to scanned + 1
                if (title of t as text) contains searchTerm then
                    focus s
                    select t
                    set index of w to 1
                    set found to true
                    exit repeat
                end if
            end repeat
            if found then exit repeat
        end repeat
        if found then exit repeat
    end repeat
    activate

        return "matched scanned=" & scanned
    else
        return "no-match scanned=" & scanned
    end if
end tell
tell application "Safari"
    set searchTerm to "
    set scanned to 0
    set found to false
    repeat with w in windows
        repeat with t in tabs of w
            set scanned to scanned + 1
            if (name of t as text) contains searchTerm then
                set current tab of w to t
                set index of w to 1
                set found to true
                exit repeat
            end if
        end repeat
        if found then exit repeat
    end repeat

Seam/ClaudeCodeInstaller.swift
/.seam/hooks/seam-claude-code.sh
seam-claude-code.sh
#!/bin/bash
# Seam Code Events Hook - bridges Claude Code lifecycle to Seam surface.
# Managed by Seam.app - do not edit manually.
# Reads hook JSON from stdin, posts a macOS distributed notification.
# MUST NEVER block Claude Code. All errors are swallowed and the script
# always exits 0 so a missing/broken Seam install can never break tool
# calls or surface "hook error" notices in the Claude Code transcript.
export SEAM_HOOK_INPUT="$(/bin/cat 2>/dev/null)"
/usr/bin/osascript -l JavaScript -e '
ObjC.import("Foundation");
(function() {
    try {
        var inputStr = ObjC.unwrap(
            $.NSProcessInfo.processInfo.environment.objectForKey($("SEAM_HOOK_INPUT"))
        );
        if (!inputStr) return;
        var input = JSON.parse(inputStr);
        var event = input.hook_event_name || "";
        var tool = input.tool_name || "";
        var sessionId = input.session_id || "";
        var sourceApp = ObjC.unwrap(
            $.NSProcessInfo.processInfo.environment.objectForKey($("TERM_PROGRAM"))
        ) || "";
        var stage = "";
        var message = "";
        if (event === "UserPromptSubmit") {
            // Marks the start of a turn. Without this hook the working
            // state cannot show during pure-thinking time before the
            // first tool call.
            stage = "thinking";
        } else if (event === "PreToolUse") {
            if (["Edit", "Write", "MultiEdit", "Bash"].indexOf(tool) >= 0) {
                stage = "writing";
                message = tool;
            } else if (["Read", "Grep", "Glob", "Agent", "Explore"].indexOf(tool) >= 0) {
                stage = "analyzing";
                message = tool;
            } else {
                stage = "thinking";
                message = tool;
            }
        } else if (event === "PostToolUse") {
            // PostToolUse keeps the activity alive between tool calls.
            stage = "thinking";
            message = tool;
        } else if (event === "Stop") {
            stage = "completed";
        }
        if (stage) {
            var dnc = $.NSDistributedNotificationCenter.defaultCenter;
            var info = $.NSMutableDictionary.alloc.init;
            info.setObjectForKey($(stage), $("stage"));
            if (message) info.setObjectForKey($(message), $("message"));
            if (sessionId) info.setObjectForKey($(sessionId), $("sessionID"));
            if (sourceApp) info.setObjectForKey($(sourceApp), $("sourceApp"));
            dnc.postNotificationNameObjectUserInfoDeliverImmediately(
                $("app.seam.claudecode.event"), $("seam"), info, true
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {}
})();
' >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
# Seam Code Events Hook - bridges Codex lifecycle to Seam surface.
# Managed by Seam.app - do not edit manually.
# Handles both legacy notify (payload in $1) and new [hooks] (stdin).
# MUST NEVER block Codex. All errors are swallowed and the script
# always exits 0 so a missing/broken Seam install can never break
# Codex tool calls.
if [ -t 0 ]; then
    SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD="$1"
    SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD="$(/bin/cat 2>/dev/null)"
    if [ -z "$SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD" ] && [ -n "$1" ]; then
        SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD="$1"
    fi
if [ -z "$SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD" ]; then
    exit 0
export SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD
/usr/bin/osascript -l JavaScript -e '
ObjC.import("Foundation");
(function() {
    try {
        var inputStr = ObjC.unwrap(
            $.NSProcessInfo.processInfo.environment.objectForKey($("SEAM_CODEX_PAYLOAD"))
        );
        if (!inputStr) return;
        var input = JSON.parse(inputStr);
        var event = input.hook_event_name || input.type || "";
        var sessionId = input.session_id || input["thread-id"] || "";
        var tool = input.tool_name || "";
        var stage = "";
        var message = "";
        if (event === "UserPromptSubmit") {
            stage = "thinking";
        } else if (event === "PreToolUse") {
            // Codex tool names vary across releases. Map a few
            // well-known ones, fall through to "thinking" otherwise.
            if (["apply_patch", "shell"].indexOf(tool) >= 0) {
                stage = "writing";
                message = tool;
            } else if (["read_file", "view_image"].indexOf(tool) >= 0) {
                stage = "analyzing";
                message = tool;
            } else {
                stage = "thinking";
                message = tool;
            }
        } else if (event === "PostToolUse") {
            stage = "thinking";
            message = tool;
        } else if (event === "Stop" || event === "agent-turn-complete") {
            stage = "completed";
            var lastMsg = input.last_assistant_message || input["last-assistant-message"] || "";
            if (lastMsg.length > 80) lastMsg = lastMsg.substring(0, 80);
            message = lastMsg;
        }
        if (stage) {
            var dnc = $.NSDistributedNotificationCenter.defaultCenter;
            var info = $.NSMutableDictionary.alloc.init;
            info.setObjectForKey($(stage), $("stage"));
            if (message) info.setObjectForKey($(message), $("message"));
            if (sessionId) info.setObjectForKey($(sessionId), $("sessionID"));
            info.setObjectForKey($("codex"), $("sourceApp"));
            dnc.postNotificationNameObjectUserInfoDeliverImmediately(
                $("app.seam.codex.event"), $("seam"), info, true
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {}
})();
' >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
exit 0

/.seam/hooks/seam-cursor-hook.sh
seam-cursor-hook.sh
beforeSubmitPrompt
#!/bin/bash
# Seam Code Events Hook - bridges Cursor agent lifecycle to Seam surface.
# Managed by Seam.app - do not edit manually.
# Reads hook JSON from stdin, posts a macOS distributed notification.
# MUST NEVER block Cursor. Cursor waits on this script before continuing its
# agent loop, so every error is swallowed and the script always exits 0.
export SEAM_HOOK_INPUT="$(/bin/cat 2>/dev/null)"
/usr/bin/osascript -l JavaScript -e '
ObjC.import("Foundation");
(function() {
    try {
        var inputStr = ObjC.unwrap(
            $.NSProcessInfo.processInfo.environment.objectForKey($("SEAM_HOOK_INPUT"))
        );
        if (!inputStr) return;
        var input = JSON.parse(inputStr);
        var event = input.hook_event_name || "";
        var tool = input.tool_name || "";
        var sessionId = input.conversation_id || "";
        var stage = "";
        var message = "";
        if (event === "beforeSubmitPrompt") {
            // Marks the start of a turn, the only signal that arrives before
            // the first tool call.
            stage = "thinking";
        } else if (event === "afterFileEdit") {
            stage = "writing";
        } else if (event === "preToolUse") {
            if (["Shell", "Edit", "Write", "MultiEdit", "apply_patch"].indexOf(tool) >= 0) {
                stage = "writing";
                message = tool;
            } else if (["Read", "Search", "Grep", "Glob", "List", "Codebase"].indexOf(tool) >= 0) {
                stage = "analyzing";
                message = tool;
            } else {
                stage = "thinking";
                message = tool;
            }
        } else if (event === "postToolUse") {
            // Keeps the activity alive between tool calls.
            stage = "thinking";
            message = tool;
        } else if (event === "stop") {
            // status is completed, aborted or error. Only a real error is a
            // failure, an aborted turn is the user stopping the agent.
            stage = input.status === "error" ? "failed" : "completed";
        }
        if (stage) {
            var dnc = $.NSDistributedNotificationCenter.defaultCenter;
            var info = $.NSMutableDictionary.alloc.init;
            info.setObjectForKey($(stage), $("stage"));
            if (message) info.setObjectForKey($(message), $("message"));
            if (sessionId) info.setObjectForKey($(sessionId), $("sessionID"));
            info.setObjectForKey($("cursor"), $("sourceApp"));
            dnc.postNotificationNameObjectUserInfoDeliverImmediately(
                $("app.seam.cursor.event"), $("seam"), info, true
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {}
})();
' >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
exit 0

<!DOCTYPE html>

<script>
var tag=document.createElement('script');
tag.src='https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api';
document.head.appendChild(tag);
var player;
function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady(){
    player=new YT.Player('player',{
        height:'1',width:'1',
        playerVars:{autoplay:0,controls:0,disablekb:1,fs:0,modestbranding:1,rel:0,origin:'http://localhost'},
        events:{
            onReady:function(){post('ready',{})},
            onStateChange:function(e){
                if(e.data==1&&shouldSeekRandom){
                    shouldSeekRandom=false;
                    var dur=player.getDuration();
                    var minStart=dur*0.05;
                    var maxStart=dur*0.25;
                    var startAt=Math.floor(minStart+Math.random()*(maxStart-minStart));
                    player.seekTo(startAt,true);
                    post('seeked',{position:startAt,duration:dur});
                }
                post('state',{state:e.data});
            },
            onError:function(e){post('error',{code:e.data})}
        }
    });
var shouldSeekRandom=false;
function loadVideo(id,vol){
    if(!player)return;
    player.setVolume(vol);
    shouldSeekRandom=true;
    player.loadVideoById(id);
function pauseVideo(){if(player)player.pauseVideo()}
function resumeVideo(){if(player)player.playVideo()}
function setPlayerVolume(v){if(player)player.setVolume(v)}
function post(type,data){
    window.webkit.messageHandlers.ytPlayer.postMessage(JSON.stringify({type:type,data:data}));
</script>
</body></html>
